FFB TRANSIENT RESIDUAL -- BLIND TRACE of the fuel sum 0x02FFE0, the writer 0x030C38 of [0xFFFF73B4] and
the writers of every other addend (corrections item 109 follow-up)
==================================================================================================================
Date            2026-11-27
Reference bin   rom/AE5L600L 20g rev 20.19c.bin      md5 92cae8275cd4f9b473a3a9e36efe6449
                (stock rom/ae5l600l.bin md5 a8ea39d447f977e270e27ee670243c88 and rev 20.19e compared for every
                scalar and record used: emu P4 / P6)
Method          Dumped blind with scripts/mapping/fn_dump.py (no labels) from each real entry, read from the bytes,
                then run in scripts/sh2e_emu.py against models written from the listings:
                scripts/trace/emu_ffb_residual.py, exit 0, 6,633 asserted checks (passes P1-P10; P10 = audit).
Revision        Third, deciding decode after an adversarial review (2026-11-27). Six claims were disputed; every
                change is listed in REVIEW HISTORY at the end. Multi-call emulation (P8) and call-site checks (P9)
                were added; the [7A14] threshold (624, not 1250), the [798C] reset writer 0x03602A, the runner of
                each addend writer and the [6354] input of the [7400] multiplier were corrected from the bytes.
EVIDENCE CLASS  bytes + emulated for 0x02FFE0, 0x02FF7C/8E/A2/74, 0x030C38 with 0x0312BC 0x0312DE 0x030F02
                0x03110C and the getters 0x023E48 0x01CF5E 0x021016 (one call: exact write set and values, every
                instruction address executed; many calls on one emulator: P8, P10), the initialiser 0x030B68 (P8),
                the consumer 0x0301E4, the [73B4] reader 0x032CE8 (P9), the [73D0] reader 0x01FE46 from 01FF68 to
                020012 (P10, audit), and the addend writers 0x036B48,
                0x038C50/0x038D4C, 0x036C48 and the [798C] reset 0x03602A (P6). The other addend writers are
                bounded from their STORE instructions and the shipped tables only (bytes, not emulated) -- said
                at each one.
                Identity: only where a definition names a table by its DATA pointer (scripts/defs.py d.at).
                Every RAM cell is a neutral handle [NNNN] = 0xFFFFNNNN. The runtime library and the batch-2
                files (fn_038E64_trace.txt, fn_0382C2_trace.txt) are used by their CONTRACT sections only.
                Disclosure: CLAUDE.md was in context; it names 0xFFFF6350 ECT, 0xFFFF6624 RPM, 0xFFFF63F8 engine
                load, 0xFFFF620C manifold pressure, 0xFFFF65FC vehicle speed, 0xFFFF67EC "uint16 DTC counter".
                The census label_claim field printed "task10_clol_gate_update", "fuel_dispatch_tableB_runner",
                "fuel_dispatch_tableA_runner", "stubpool_target_16", "sched_periodic_dispatch" for the runners.
                None of these is used as a premise below; where a handle is written next to one of those
                names it is labelled "(CLAUDE.md name)".
Rounding        The models round every fadd/fsub/fmul toward zero from the exact value and treat fmac as fused,
                as the emulator does (corrections item 106). NO statement below depends on the last bit of a
                float (open-holes #11: the run-time FPSCR is not settled).

ANSWER, IN ONE PARAGRAPH
  0x02FFE0 forms S = [73B4] + [7A08] + [7A14] + [7AF4] + [7B8C] + 1.0 - [7A44] + [7BCC] + [7BA8]
  + max( min([798C] + [7A20], 0.52), min([73A4] + [7BE4], 0.65) + [7354] + [7374] ) and stores
  clamp(S * [7340], 0, 32) to [730C] and clamp((S + [7B44+4i] + [7A78+4i]) * [7340], 0, 32) to [7310+4i]
  (byte 0x0CBBD2 = 0 in stock / 20.19c / 20.19e; the other branch is decoded and emulated but dead).
  [7340] is 1.0 unless byte [8F24] == 1 (then 1 + s8([8F08])/100 in [1.0, 1.1999999]). The consumer 0x0301E4
  multiplies [730C] by X = ([76D4] + [7878] + [7AE4]) * [7348] and clamps to [[733C], 262136].
  0x030C38 IS the writer of [73B4]: [73B4] = (T1 + T2 + T3) * [7400], gated, where
  T1 = f([73EC]) * g([73B0]) * tauA([6350]) * h(RPM), [73EC] = the two-call change of [73B0] = 0.01*[620C]
  with an RPM deadband 0.06..0.10 and clamp +-10; T2 = 0.02 * [73D8] * k([6350], [73B0]) (rising),
  [73D8] = [73B0] minus a slow first-order lag of it (k = 0.014 per call), deadband +-0.05; T3 = 0 in
  this calibration. tauA is one of three planes of the table the definition names "Tau Input A Rising
  Load Activation" (data 0x0CD6E6) -- 0.25 / 0.30 / 0.50 at 90 C in 20.19c. [7400] is 1.0 once its seed
  has decayed; the seed is taken from [6354] (a different cell from [6350]): 1.25 at [6354] >= 80 (back to
  1.0 after 750 calls), up to 4.9502 at [6354] = 0 (back to 1.0 after 3600 calls while [6354] stays >= 0;
  1965 if it is at -30 -- the decay interval follows the current [6354]). No clamp bounds [73B4] above
  (+15.92 emulated cold); below it is >= 0 while [67EC] < 875. With the 20.19c tables and
  [7400] = 1.0 a warm (90 C) step of [73B0] by 0.20 / 0.25 in one call gives [73B4] = +0.059 / +0.082 on
  that call AND the next ([73EC] spans two calls), then a T2 tail of about 0.004 for about 100 calls
  (emulated, P8). A one-call step of 2.0 gives +1.307 with [7400] = 1.0 (best of a model sweep), +1.633
  with [7400] = 1.25 and +6.47 with [7400] = 4.9502 (emulated): the sweep maximum is not a bound.
  A rise just after a fall can take the snap branch of 0x0312DE, which gives a larger and longer T2 tail
  than the same rise from settled followers (audit, P10). The tree's lag state [73D0] is also read by
  0x01FE46, which sets the byte [641C] that switches the 0x0301E4 lower clamp [733C] (audit, P10).
  Of the other addends: [7AF4] = 0 (item 108), [7B8C] <= +0.046875 (item 109), [7A08] = 0 warm
  (its table is 0 for coolant >= 10 C), [7BCC] (0.15) and [7BA8] (<= 0.03) are forced to 0 whenever the
  open-loop enrichment [798C] is non-zero, [7A14] (<= 0.3) and [7A20] (0.1 / 0.2) are written by stores
  that read no [620C] / [73B0] rate, and the per-cylinder terms do not enter [730C]. [7A44] has no writer
  that three searches can find. Several addends are written by runners OTHER than 0x04AA58 (P9), so the
  sum can combine values from different passes. AMONG THE ADDENDS WHOSE RANGE IS ESTABLISHED FROM BYTES,
  only [73B4] has a rate-of-change input and can supply the observed +0.05..+0.098 by itself; [7B8C] can
  add at most +0.047. [7354], [7374], [7A44] and the [79C0] scaling of [798C] are NOT established, so
  they are not excluded; [798C] itself follows the level of an OL map of ([63F8], [6624]).

T-BIT SENSES RELIED ON (n = bits 11-8, m = bits 7-4 of the word)
    fcmp/gt FRm,FRn   T = (FRn > FRm); NaN makes it 0. Examples: F985 at 030F5A: T = (fr9 > fr8) = (0.0 > [73EC]);
                      F8E5 at 0312FC: T = (fr8 > fr14) = (10.0 > d); FE85 at 030CE2: T = (fr14 > fr8) = (0.605 > [73D8])
    cmp/ge  Rm,Rn     T = (Rn >= Rm) signed. 3E63 at 030D0A: T = (r14 >= r6) = ([67EC] >= threshold), both
                      zero-extended 16-bit, so the order is unsigned 16-bit
    cmp/hs  Rm,Rn     T = (Rn >= Rm) unsigned. 3602 at 03125C: T = (r6 >= r0) = ([7441] >= [7440]); both are
                      sign-extended bytes, which keeps the unsigned byte order
    cmp/eq  #imm,r0   T = (r0 == imm). On a sign-extended byte, "== 1" means the byte is exactly 0x01
    tst Rm,Rn         T = ((Rn & Rm) == 0); tst #imm,r0: T = ((r0 & imm) == 0)
    bt / bf           branch when T = 1 / T = 0; bt/s and bf/s execute the delay slot on both paths
    fadd FRm,FRn      FRn = FRn + FRm; fsub FRm,FRn: FRn = FRn - FRm; fmul FRm,FRn: FRn = FRn * FRm
    fmac FR0,FRm,FRn  FRn = FR0 * FRm + FRn
    sh2e_disasm prints displacements already in bytes.

;  [73B8] [73BC] [73C0] [73C4] [73C8] [73CC] [7414]  f32  lookups / RPM copy  030C38
;
;  Other writers of this block (store scan; bytes, not emulated -- listings in SUPPORTING DECODES):
;    0x030B68 (initialiser, called from 0x047C40 at 047F28; when 0x047C40 takes that path is not
;      established here): [73E0] = 0x0ACD7C([6354]), [73E4] = 0x0ACD90([6354]),
;      [7400] = [73E0] + [73E4] (no clamp), [73B0] (0x0312BC), [73D0] = [73D4] = [7408..7410] = [73B0]
;      (emulated, P8). find_writers lists no other writer of [73E0] / [73E4] than 0x030B68 and 0x03110C.
;    0x030BCA (from 0x04907C): when byte [65F6] == 1 (0x022F92): [73B0] refreshed, [73D0] = [73D4] =
;      [7408..7410] = [73B0], byte [7446] = 0 -- the next 0x030C38 call then starts from zero deltas.
;    0x030C38 rewrites [7400] through 0x03110C before every use, so 0x030B68's unclamped sum never reaches
;    [73B4].
;    0x030B68 does NOT touch the decay counter byte [7441] (no store to it in 030B68-030BC8): the first
;      decay step after a seed comes when [7441] reaches the hold count, so the decay course shifts with
;      whatever [7441] holds (audit, emu P10: [7441] = 10 at the seed -> first 1.0 at call 740, not 750).
;    0x030C10 (from 0x04907C; audit): word [7442] = saturating [7442] + 1 (0x0BE554) when byte [7C9D] == 1,
;      else 0 (030C12-030C30). Its only reader here is the hold test at 0313D8, which with 0x0ACD48 = 0
;      passes for every value, so [7442] does not matter in this calibration.
;
;  READERS OUTSIDE THE TREE of cells it writes (audit: whole-ROM access scan, every census function,
;  base registers and GBR propagated from the entry; a LOWER BOUND like the store scan below):
;    [73B4]  0x02FFE0, 0x0243B6, 0x032CE8 only (as stated in the 0x030C38 CONTRACT).
;    [73D0]  0x01FE46 (01FE8C/01FE8E, runner 0x04AA58 at 04AA80, i.e. BEFORE 0x030C38 at 04AACE, so it sees
;            the previous pass's value): byte [641C] = [641A] = 1 only when [73D0] >= [6404], where [6404] =
;            0x0AAE64(RPM) (10.0 above 4000 rpm, 13.0 at 4000) when [65FC] >= 50.0 (0x0C3144), else
;            0x0AAE78(RPM) = 32.0; with [6350] >= 70 (0x0C3138), RPM >= 4400 (0x0C313C), [62DC] >= 30
;            (0x0C3140), [62E4] < -4.0 (0x0C3148) and one of byte [65C5] == 1, byte [5E94] == 4, byte [65C0]
;            == 1 (or byte [5E94] == 0 with byte 0x0C302F == 90; it is 0). Otherwise [641A] = 0 and [641C] is
;            cleared unless [62E4] < 0.0, RPM >= 3000, byte [7C9D] != 1 and byte [641B] != 0 (latch held).
;            (01FF68-020010; emulated, P10.) [641C] is the byte that makes [733C] = 0.0 (0x02FF8E, the lower
;            clamp of 0x0301E4) and adds the RPM factors [741C] / [7428] on the negative branches of 0x030F02,
;            so the tree's own lag state feeds back into its inputs and into the 0x0301E4 clamp one pass later.
;            Constants and both tables identical in stock / 20.19c / 20.19e (P10). Identities of [62DC],
;            [62E4], [65FC] are not used. The only other stores to [641C] are clears: 0x020678 (020708; from
;            0x047C40) and 0x020726 (02079C; from 0x04907C, on its byte-[65F6] == 1 path, 020734-02073A).
;    [7400] [73E8]  0x038E64 (038E92 / 038E96): M = ((P-1)*K_P + 1)*((Q-1)*K_Q + 1) with K_P = K_Q = 0.0
;            (verified fn_038E64_trace.txt) -> no effect for finite values.
;    [73E8]  0x03F00C (03F068; compared at 03F10A with 0x0D2A58 = 0.0) and 0x041EEE (041F12; compared at
;            041FF6 with 0x0D2C5C = 1.25) -- gates in those functions; with 0x0AC41C flat 1.0 they are fixed
;            ([73E8] > 0.0 true, > 1.25 false). Editing 0x0AC41C would move them. Not decoded further.
;    [73D8]  0x052644 (05266C/05266E -> stack slot 0; band test against 0x0D65B0 / 0x0D65B4 at 0528BA-0528CE).
;            Not decoded further.

==== FUNCTION 0x02FFE0 .. 0x030162  (193 instructions, 1 caller: 0x02FF7C at 0x02FF86) ====

CONTRACT
  inputs   RAM only: [7A20] [798C] [7B8C] [7BE4] [7A78] [7A7C] [7A80] [7A84] [7B44] [7B48] [7B4C] [7B50]
           [73B4] [7A08] [7A14] [7AF4] [73A4] [7354] [7374] [7BA8] [7A44] [7BCC] [7340] (all f32); calibration
           byte 0x0CBBD2 and f32 0x0CBE00 = 0.65, 0x0CBE04 = 0.52. On the live branch EVERY one of the 23 cells
           is read (emu P1 asserts the read set equals the list).
  outputs  [730C] [7310] [7314] [7318] [731C] (f32) and nothing else outside the stack (emu P1, exact write set).
  preserves r13 r14 PR fr12-fr15 (asserted).
  formula (live, byte 0x0CBBD2 != 90):
      B   = ((((([73B4] + [7A08]) + [7A14]) + [7AF4]) + [7B8C]) + 1.0)
      CL  = min([73A4] + [7BE4], 0.65) + ([7354] + [7374])
      OL  = min([798C] + [7A20], 0.52)
      S   = (B - [7A44]) + (([7BCC] + [7BA8]) + max(OL, CL))
      [730C]     = clamp(S * [7340], 0.0, 32.0)
      [7310+4i]  = clamp(((S + [7B44+4i]) + [7A78+4i]) * [7340], 0.0, 32.0)        i = 0, 1, 2, 3
  formula (byte 0x0CBBD2 == 90; dead in stock / 20.19c / 20.19e, emulated on an in-memory copy):
      S   = (B + CL) + (max([798C], [7BA8]) + (([7A20] - [7A44]) + [7BCC]))       (no 0.52 cap, no max with CL)
MECHANISM
  min / max / clamp are the runtime helpers 0x0BE970 / 0x0BE960 / 0x0BE56C:
    min(a, b) = a if b > a else b;  max(a, b) = a if a > b else b;  clamp(x, lo, hi) = lo if !(x > lo),
    x if hi > x, else hi.  So a NaN S gives 0.0 (the low bound); NaN in the min/max inputs returns the
    second argument (min/max) -- e.g. a NaN [798C]+[7A20] makes OL = 0.52.
  The sum is accumulated left to right in the order shown (02FFFA-030052, 0300BA-0300BC); no addend is
  scaled before [7340] ([7A44] enters with sign -1). LINEAR (always count): [73B4] [7A08] [7A14] [7AF4]
  [7B8C] [7BCC] [7BA8] and -[7A44]. INSIDE max(OL, CL) only: [798C] [7A20] (OL, capped 0.52) and [73A4]
  [7BE4] (capped 0.65) [7354] [7374] (CL) -- a change of one of these moves S only while its side is the
  larger one (and, for a capped pair, below its cap). Audit, emu P10: [798C] = 0.4 gives [730C] = 1.4 with
  [7374] = 0 and with [7374] = 0.1 (masked), 1.5 with [7374] = 0.5; [73B4] = 0.08 adds 0.08 in every case.
  The per-cylinder terms [7B44+4i] and [7A78+4i] enter ONLY the cylinder outputs, never [730C].
  [7340] multiplies the whole of (S + cylinder terms).
EDGE CASES (emu P1)
  * S*[7340] <= 0 or NaN -> 0.0; >= 32 -> 32.0 (asserted with S = -100 / +100).
  * OL vs CL: [798C] = 0.3359375, CL = 0 -> 730C = 1.3359375; [798C] = 0.6 -> OL capped at 0.52 -> 1.52;
    [798C] = 0.25 with [73A4] = 0.75 -> CL = min(0.75, 0.65) = 0.65 wins -> 1.65 (all asserted).
EVIDENCE   pool 0x03018C..0x0301DC (every RAM literal), 030040-030052 (B), 030056-03006C (CL, min 03005E with
           fr5 = 0.65 in the slot), 030070-030074 (branch), 030096-0300BC (live branch), 0300C0-03014E (products
           and clamps; r13 = 0xFFFF7340 so @(r0,r13) with r0 = -52 / -48 / -44 / -40 / -36 is 730C / 7310 / 7314
           / 7318 / 731C). emulated: P1, 1,400 cases (700 per branch), all 193 addresses executed.

==== FUNCTION 0x0312DE .. 0x0314D6  (181 instructions, 1 caller: 0x030C38 at 0x030CAE) ====

CONTRACT
  d = [7408] - [7410]                                     (= [73B0] now minus [73B0] two calls ago)
  [73EC] = 10.0                          if !(10.0 > d)   (d >= 10 or NaN)
         = 0.0                           if ACD34(RPM) > |d|        (RPM = [7414]; 0.060 at <= 800 rpm .. 0.100 at >= 3200)
         = -10.0                         if -10.0 > d
         = d                             otherwise
  hold   = (byte [7C9D] == 1 and u16 [7442] >= 0x0ACD48([6350]) (= 0 -> always))
           or (byte [7C9A] >= 3) or (byte [7445] == 1)
  if hold:                         [73D0] = [73D4] = [73B0]
  elif the PREVIOUS [73D8] >= 0 (or NaN):          filter     (the -19.5 test at 031402 can never pass:
                                                               [73EC] >= -10)
  elif [73EC] < 0.975:                              filter
  else:                            [73D0] = [73D4] = [740C]   (the previous [73B0])
  filter: if [73B0] < [73D0]:  [73D0] = lag([73B0], [73D0], 0.08);  [73D4] = lag([73B0], [73D4], 0.015)
          else:                [73D0] = lag([73B0], [73D0], 0.014); [73D4] = lag([73B0], [73D4], 0.014)
          lag(A, B, k) = helper 0x0BEA40 = A + (1-k)(B-A), snapped to A when |A - result| < 1.831e-4,
          A returned when B is not finite.
  [73D8] = [73B0] - [73D0], set to 0.0 when -0.05 <= value < 0.05
  [73DC] = [73B0] - [73D4]          (band [0.0, 0.0) is empty: never zeroed in this calibration)
MECHANISM  as the CONTRACT. With k = 0.014 a step of [73B0] by D gives [73D8] = D - 0.014*D = 0.986 D on the
           step call and decays by a factor 0.986 per call while [73B0] holds (emu P5 prints 0.1479 for D = 0.15).
           [73EC] responds only while the two-call difference exceeds the RPM deadband: it is a rate signal;
           [73D8] is a "distance from a slow follower" signal.
           The snap branch (031416-03141E) is a LIFT-THEN-TIP-IN path: after a fall has left [73D8] < -0.05,
           a rise with a two-call change >= 0.975 resets both followers to the PREVIOUS [73B0], so [73D8]
           jumps to the one-call step instead of to the step minus a lagged follower, and the T2 tail
           restarts from there (audit, emu P10 against a ROM copy with 0x0CBE5C = 1000.0, i.e. no snap:
           E 90, [73B0] 2.0 -> 1.0 for 4 calls -> 2.2: [73D8] 1.200 vs 0.477, [73B4] on the third call 0.0233
           vs 0.0093, summed over the tail 2.643 vs 1.610, non-zero for 226 vs 160 calls; E 70: sum 9.375 vs
           5.240). The first two calls are dominated by T1 and change little (0.5240 vs 0.5095).
EVIDENCE   0312EC-0312F6 (d), 0312FA-031320 + 0313B0-0313BE ([73EC]), 0313C4-0313F2 (hold), 0313F4-031416
           (selection), 031420-031476 (filter; bt/s at 031438 on 0.0 > [73B0]-[73D0]), 031482-031488,
           03148A-0314A8 ([73D8]), 0314AA-0314C8 ([73DC]). emulated: P3.

==== FUNCTION 0x03110C .. 0x0312BC  (216 instructions, 1 caller: 0x030C38 at 0x030CB6; GBR 0xFFFF73E0) ====

CONTRACT
  [73FC] = 0x0CBE60 (1.0) if byte [7444] == 1 else 1.0.
  if [73E0] within 1 +- 2^-13 and [73E4] within 0 +- 2^-13 and byte [7445] == 0:  [7400] = 1.0, [7441] = 0.
  else [7434] = 0x0AC43C([6354]) (= 1.0) and
    [67EC] < 13:   [73E0], [73E4] = 0x0ACDA4, 0x0ACDB8 of [6354] if byte [3158] == 1, else 0x0ACD7C, 0x0ACD90;
                   [7400] = M([73E0] + [73E4]); [7441] = 0
    [67EC] >= 13:  [7400] = 1.0 if [73F0] < 0 or [73F4] < 0, else M([73E0] + [73E4]);
                   [7440] = 0x0AC430([6354]) (fixed u8: 8 / 15); [7441] = min([7441] + 1, 255);
                   when [7441] >= [7440]: [73E0] = max([73E0] - 0x0ACD54*[73FC], 1.0) (step 0.005),
                   [73E4] = max([73E4] - 0x0ACD68*[73FC], 0.0) (step 0.045), [7441] = 0
    M(s) = min(s, 8.0) if byte [647C] != 1 (selector 2), else clamp(s * [7434], 1.0, 8.0).
  EVERY table here is read at [6354] (fr14, 031120) -- a different cell from the [6350] ("E") that the
  0x030C38 tables use. So the seed follows [6354], not [6350].
  Order of the tests: the settled shortcut (03114E-03117C) comes BEFORE the [67EC] < 13 test, so a block
  that has settled ([73E0] = 1, [73E4] = 0) with byte [7445] == 0 is never re-seeded, whatever [67EC] is;
  ANY non-zero byte [7445] (tst r0,r0 / bf/s at 031170/031172, not "== 1") blocks the shortcut, and then
  [67EC] < 13 re-seeds from the tables (emu P8: settled, [67EC] = 0, [6354] = 0 -> no re-seed with byte
  [726C] = 0, re-seed to 2.2002 + 2.75 with [726C] = 1 or 2).
  Seed and decay (emu P8, 0x030B68 then 0x030C38 repeatedly on one emulator, no transient):
    * The seed is written by 0x030B68 (0x0ACD7C / 0x0ACD90 of [6354]) and re-read at the CURRENT [6354] on
      every call while [67EC] < 13 and the block is not settled; from the first call with [67EC] >= 13 on
      it is held and only decays (the seed VALUE no longer follows [6354]; but the decay INTERVAL is the
      hold count 0x0AC430 read at the CURRENT [6354] on every call: 15 calls at >= 0, 8 at <= -10).
    * Seed at [6354] >= 80: [73E0] = 1.25, [73E4] = 0 -> [7400] = 1.25, first exactly 1.0 at call 750
      (50 decrements of 0.0050049, one every 15 calls) -- counted from [7441] = 0 at the seed; 0x030B68
      does not reset [7441] (audit, P10: [7441] = 10 -> call 740).
    * Seed at [6354] = 0: 2.2001953 + 2.75 = 4.9501953 -> [7400] 4.6501 at call 100, 3.2996 at 500,
      1.8699 at 1000 (with [6354] = [6350] = 85 from call 100 on), above 1.25 until call 2849 and first
      exactly 1.0 at call 3600 -- for any later [6354] >= 0 (60 gives the same course). With [6354] = -30
      from call 100 (hold count 8) the same seed is above 1.25 until call 1564 and 1.0 at call 1965
      (audit, P10). At [6354] = -5 the seed is 3.4751.
    * [7400] is computed BEFORE that call's decrement. While [67EC] >= 13 it is 1.0 for any call with
      [73F0] < 0 or [73F4] < 0 while the decay continues underneath -- including a MIXED call with a
      positive [73EC] (positive T1) and [73D8] < 0 (audit, P10: seed 1.25, [73EC] +0.30, [73D8] -0.44 ->
      [7400] = 1.0). While [67EC] < 13 the re-seed path (03118A-0311F2) does not test [73F0] / [73F4]:
      [7400] = M(seed) on a falling call (P10: 1.25 at [67EC] = 5); [73B4] is 0 there anyway, because its
      gate needs [67EC] >= 25 (latch set) or 625.
    * Range with the 20.19c seed tables: [7400] in [1.0, 4.9502] (the code caps at 8.0). That [67EC]
      reaching 13 marks time since start is not established here.

==== REACHABLE RANGE OF [73B4] WITH THE SHIPPED (20.19c) TABLES ====

  Structure: [73B4] = (T1 + T2 + 0) * [7400], each factor bounded by its table:
    T1 = [7418]([73EC]) * ACCBC([73B0]) * [73E8] * ACBA4(RPM) * tauA(E)                  ([73EC] >= 0)
         [7418] in [0, 2.0] for [73EC] >= 0 (2.0 reached at [73EC] >= 1.0), ACCBC in [0.30, 1.0] (1.0 for
         [73B0] <= 3), [73E8] = ACBA4 = 1.0;  tauA at E = 80 / 90 / >= 100:
           0x0ACB68 (sel 0, byte [647C] == 1)          0.300 / 0.250 / 0.250
           0x0ACB54 (sel 2, byte [844F] != 1)          0.350 / 0.300 / 0.300
           0x0ACB40 (sel 2, byte [844F] == 1)          0.600 / 0.500 / 0.500
         -> T1 <= 2.0 * 1.0 * tauA: <= 0.60 / 0.70 / 1.20 for the three planes at E >= 80 (emu P5).
         [73EC] = 0 unless the two-call change exceeds ACD34(RPM): 0.060 (<= 800 rpm), 0.080 (1600),
         0.090 (2400), 0.100 (>= 3200). [7418] at [73EC] = 0.06 / 0.08 / 0.10 / 0.20 / 0.30:
         0.030 / 0.040 / 0.050 / 0.220 / 0.400.
    T2 = [73D8] * 0.02 * [742C](= 1) * [73E8](= 1) * ACBF4(= 1) * k(E, [73B0])            ([73D8] >= 0)
         k = 0x0AD82C / 0x0AD7F4 / 0x0AD810 (identical): 1.0 at E >= 90; 1.20..2.70 at 80 C; up to 12.1 cold.
         [73D8] is at most the size of the recent rise of [73B0] (slow follower, k = 0.014 per call); its
         absolute ceiling depends on the range of [620C], which these bytes do not bound.
    [7400] = 1.0 once its seed has decayed; 1.25 -> 1.0 over 750 calls for a seed taken at [6354] >= 80;
         up to 4.9502 -> 1.0 over 3600 calls for a seed taken at [6354] = 0 (0x03110C CONTRACT, emu P8).
         The seed is read at [6354], not at the [6350] used for E in this section.
    No instruction in 0x030C38 bounds [73B4] from above; 0x02FFE0 clamps the final product at 32.
  Emulated / modelled examples (warm, every gate open, settled lag states, [7400] = 1 unless stated):
    E = 90, RPM 3000, [73B0] 2.00 -> 2.15 in one call, sel 0:  [73EC] 0.150  [73D8] 0.148  [73B4] +0.037
    E = 90, RPM 3000, [73B0] 2.00 -> 2.20 in one call, sel 0:  [73EC] 0.200  [73D8] 0.197  [73B4] +0.059
    E = 90, RPM 3000, [73B0] 2.00 -> 2.25 in one call, sel 0:  [73EC] 0.250  [73D8] 0.247  [73B4] +0.082
    model sweep, one-call steps up to 2.0, E 80..110, three selector states, [7400] = 1.0: max [73B4] =
    +1.307 (sel 2 with [844F] == 1, E = 80, [73B0] 1.0 -> 3.0; T1 = 1.20, T2 = 0.107), re-emulated (P5).
    The SAME case emulated with [73E0] = 1.25 ([7400] = 1.25): +1.633; with the [6354] = 0 seed
    ([7400] = 4.9502): +6.468 (emu P5). The sweep maximum is conditional on [7400] = 1.0 and is NOT an
    upper bound; colder E also raises tauA (to 3.40) and k (to 12.1). Audit, emu P10: E = 40, the 844F
    plane, seed taken at [6354] = 0 ([7400] = 4.9502), [73B0] 1.0 -> 3.0 in one call: [73B4] = +15.92 on
    that call. The only ceiling is the 32.0 clamp of 0x02FFE0 on the whole sum.
  Time course (emulated on one emulator over many calls, P8; E = 90, RPM 3000, sel 0, [7400] = 1):
    * a one-call step of [73B0] held afterwards gives almost the full value on TWO calls, because [73EC] =
      [7408] - [7410] spans two calls: step 0.20 -> [73B4] 0.0590, 0.0589, then 0.0038 ([73EC] back to 0);
      step 0.25 -> 0.0824, 0.0824, then 0.0048.
    * the T2 tail ([73D8] decaying by 0.986 per call down to the 0.05 band) keeps [73B4] non-zero for 98
      calls after the 0.20 step and 114 calls after the 0.25 step.
    * a ramp of +0.02 per call for 60 calls never leaves the [73EC] deadband (two-call change 0.04 < 0.10):
      [73EC] = 0 on every call, yet [73D8] reaches 0.804 and [73B4] 0.0161 through T2 alone.
    * all of the above start from SETTLED followers. A rise that comes shortly after a fall (previous
      [73D8] < -0.05) with a two-call change >= 0.975 takes the snap branch of 0x0312DE: [73D8] = the full
      one-call step and a longer, larger T2 tail (0x0312DE MECHANISM; audit, emu P10: tail sum 2.643 vs
      1.610 and 226 vs 160 non-zero calls against a no-snap ROM copy, E 90).
  Negative values: on falling [73B0] ([73EC] < 0, [73D8] < 0) both terms are negative; they reach [73B4]
  only once [67EC] >= 875 (before that max(.., 0) clips them). The two signs can also differ on one call
  (a small rise just after a fall: T1 > 0, T2 < 0); then [73F4] < 0 forces [7400] = 1.0 (while [67EC] >= 13),
  so the positive T1 is not multiplied by a live seed (audit, emu P10: seed 1.25, [73B4] = +0.09998 with
  [7400] = 1.0).
  Other outputs of the tree, from the same tables (20.19c):
    [73B0] = 0.01*[620C] (range set by [620C], not bounded here)
    [73EC] = 0, or a value in [-10, -th] U [th, 10] with th = 0x0ACD34(RPM) in [0.06, 0.10]
    [73D8] = 0, or |value| >= 0.05 (no table bound; follows the size of recent [73B0] changes)
    [73DC] unbounded by tables (no band)          [73F8] = 0.0 (finite inputs)
    [73F0] in [-0.2998*3.0*1.953, 2.0*1.0*1.0] = [-1.757, 2.0]
    [73F4] = 0.02*[73D8] ([73D8] >= 0) or 0.3*[73D8] (*0.977 when byte [641C] == 1) ([73D8] < 0)
    [7400] in [1.0, 4.9502] once seeded with the 20.19c seed tables (code cap 8.0; [73E0] >= 1.0 and [73E4]
         >= 0 after every decay step; seeds >= 1.25)
    [73B8] one tauA / falling-tau cell value (0 .. 3.40); [73C4] in [1.0, 1.15]; [73BC] in [0, 12.11];
    [73C8] in [0.5, 1.0]; [73C0] = [73CC] = 1.0; [7404] in {25, 625}; [7446] in {0, 1} after the first call
  Steady state: [73EC] = 0 (deadband) and [73D8] = 0 (band +-0.05) -> [73B4] = 0. So [73B4] is a
  TRANSIENT-only term: it is non-zero only while [73B0] changes faster than 0.06..0.10 per two calls or
  sits more than 0.05 away from its slow follower (the second condition persists for ~100 calls after a
  step and is reached by a slow ramp that never trips the first, P8).

==== OTHER ADDENDS: WRITERS AND REACHABLE RANGES ====

  Writers were found by a whole-ROM store scan over every census function with the base register value
  propagated forward from the function entry (function_census.reg_states; direct, @(disp,Rn), @(r0,Rn),
  @-Rn, GBR forms). That scan is a LOWER BOUND: 12,523 stores in 1,919 functions have an unresolved base.
  find_writers.py was not usable as the primary tool (it misses [73B4] itself). The first version of this
  section still omitted the [798C] reset 0x03602A, which find_writers does list: both scans are lower bounds.

  [7AF4]  writers 0x0382C2 (0387A4..038842) and the reset 0x038860 (0388A0 stores 0.0). Verified file
          fn_0382C2_trace.txt: never non-zero. Range {0}.
  [7B8C]  writers 0x038E64 (0391D0) and 0x0392C4 (0392E2). Verified file fn_038E64_trace.txt / corrections
          item 109: S <= +0.046875 with the shipped tables, >= lo1 + lo2 = -0.1999512.
  [7A08]  writer 0x036B48 (tail of 0x036AAE, called at 04AAE0). Decoded and emulated (P6, 504 cases):
          on a rising edge of byte [7A0C] (previous [7A10] == 0, now == 1) [7A08] = 0x0AC620([6350]);
          while [7A0C] == 1 and |[7A08]| > 2^-11 it steps down by 0.004 (0x0CC230) per call, floored at 0;
          otherwise 0.0. 0x0AC620 = 0.30, 0.30, 0.30, 0.20, 0.10 at -40..0 C and 0 for E >= 10 C.
          Range [0, 0.30078]; exactly 0 for a warm engine unless the edge happened below 10 C less than
          76 calls earlier. Not driven by a load change. (writer of the flag [7A0C]: 0x036A98 / 0x036AB2, not
          decoded)
  [7A14]  writers 0x036BF4 (036C36, called from 0x047C40) and 0x036C48 (036CFA / 036D04, via 0x036C3C from
          0x049CF0) -- NEITHER runner is 0x04AA58. 0x036C48 (emulated P6, 500 cases):
          [7A14] = min([7A18], 0.15) when u16 [67EC] >= 624 (word 0x0CBC6E; 036CD6-036CE0) and byte [85D7]
          == 0, else [7A18]. (The first version of this file said 1250: that is word 0x0CBC70, the [7A20]
          gate of 0x036E86 at 036EE6.) Before that, unless byte [726C] == 1 or byte [65F6] == 1, it adds 1
          (saturating) to word [7A1C] and raises it to at least 188 (word 0x0CBC6A) when the CL-side addend
          [7374] is within +-2^-12 of 0 (0x0BE608 at 036C92) and byte [6552] == 1 (0x021D9A returns 2).
          0x036BF4 sets [7A18] = 0.3 when [6350] >= 79 and [6364] >= 0.0; else 0.0 when [6350] >= 70 and
          [6364] >= 0.0; else leaves it (a single-call two-threshold set, then copies it to [7A14]).
          0x036D14 (bra from 0x036C40, runner 0x04A6FA): byte [7C18] == 1 -> [7A18] = max([7A18] - 0.3, 0);

          floor of 0.2 when [7A18] > 0, u16 [67EC] < 11250 (0x0CBC6C) and two getter results (0x021D9A,
          0x029808) are 0, else to 0. So the timing of the step-downs depends on [7374] through [7A1C].
          Range [0, 0.3] if [7A18] starts from a value 0x036BF4 stores (its power-on value is not
          established). No store in these three functions reads [620C], [73B0] or [63F8]; whether [7374]

  OL side  min([798C] + [7A20], 0.52):
          [798C] writers: the reset 0x03602A (03603E, emulated P6: 0.0 to [798C] [7990] [7994] [7998] [799C]

          tables the definitions name "Primary Open Loop Fueling (KCA Alternate Mode)" (0x0AD674, data
          0x0CFD30) / "(Failsafe)(KCA Alternate Mode)" (0x0AD690) with ([63F8], [6624]), the floors "Minimum
          Primary Open Loop Enrichment (Throttle)" (0x0AC5D0: 0.109 above 73.4) and "(Accelerator)"
          (0x0AC5E4: 0), zeroes a map value below 0.0637 (0x0CC170), multiplies by [79C0], and rises toward
          that target by "AFL Ramp Rate (CL to OL Transition Speed)" (0x0AD090: 0.00305 per call below
          5000 rpm) until it has caught up, then follows it immediately (03623A-036270, 0362F8-036306).
          So [798C] can lag BELOW the map while it ramps; it exceeds the map only through the 0.109 throttle
          floor (when the map is below it) or a [79C0] above 1.0 -- [79C0]'s writer is NOT decoded (the scan
          shows only word stores from 0x036322). (bytes; not emulated)
          [7A20] writers 0x036E60 (036E7C, = [7A38]; runner 0x047C40) and 0x036E86 (036F64; runner 0x04A674,
          not 0x04AA58): [7A20] = [7A38] in {0.1, 0.2}
          (0x0CC284..0x0CC290) when u16 [67EC] >= 1250, byte [7A3E] == 1, byte [7A28] == 1 and two more
          flag-or-getter conditions hold (036EEE-036F18), else 0.0; [7A38] is set to 0.2 at coolant >= 105
          with [7A34] < 12000 and back to 0.1 below 102 C or at [7A34] >= 12800. Range {0, 0.1, 0.2}. Its gates read coolant (hysteresis 100/101 C in 0x03700A),

          (0x036F76); no [620C] / [73B0] rate input in these stores. (bytes; not emulated)

          [73A4] writer 0x030ACC (030B30; runner 0x049CF0, not 0x04AA58): [73A4] = [73A8] + [73AC];
